Instead of clamping every\n// single access, the row pointers for the y/z neighbours are clamped once per row and only\n// the two x-boundary cells of a row need special treatment. This leaves a contiguous,\n// branch-free inner loop over x that the compiler can vectorize, and the (z, y) rows are\n// distributed over the OpenMP team with a static schedule so that every thread keeps\n// touching the same part of the grid across all time steps (NUMA locality).\n//\n// `body` is invoked as body(idx, laplacian) for every cell. With Recip the (loop\n// invariant) divisions by the squared grid spacings are turned into multiplications by\n// their reciprocals, which the caller only selects when that is bit-exact.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename F>\ninline void stencilLoop(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, F body) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const double dx2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    // Scale a second difference by the corresponding 1/h^2 factor.\n    const auto scale = [](const double v, const double h2) {\n        if constexpr (Recip) {\n            return v * h2;\n        } else {\n            return v / h2;\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Orphaned worksharing construct: this is called from inside a parallel region.\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ row = f + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowYp = f + z * nxy + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowYn = f + z * nxy + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : y) * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowZp = f + ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowZn = f + ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n\n            // Laplacian at position x of this row, given the clamped x neighbours.\n            const auto lap = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n                const double cv = row[x];\n                const double cxx = scale(row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n                const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n                const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n                return cxx + cyy + czz;\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(base, lap(0, 0, 0));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(base, lap(0, 1, 0));\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                const double cv = row[x];\n                const double cxx = scale(row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n                const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n                const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n                body(base + x, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n\n            body(base + nx - 1, lap(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        const double cv = c[idx];\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                 + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                 - gamma * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field. The extra buffers are first-touched with the same\n// static (z, y) distribution used by the compute kernels so that all pages end up on the\n// NUMA node of the thread that will work on them.\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ cnew, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = base + x;\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = idx;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                mu[idx] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict__ data = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i]);\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = data[0];\n    double maxVal = data[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    configureThreads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays\n    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);\n    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);\n    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(cold.data(), cnew.data(), mu.data(), nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    double* coldPtr = cold.data();\n    double* cnewPtr = cnew.data();\n    double* muPtr = mu.data();\n\n    // One single parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are created once and the\n    // per-step synchronization is just the implicit barriers of the worksharing constructs.\n    // Selected once: multiplying by the reciprocal grid spacings is only used when it\n    // yields exactly the same results as dividing.\n    const bool recip = divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dx * dx) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dy * dy) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dz * dz);\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            if (recip) {\n                computeChemicalPotential<true>(coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                               gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            } else {\n                computeChemicalPotential<false>(coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            }\n\n            // Update concentration\n            if (recip) {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<true>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            } else {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<false>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            std::swap(coldPtr, cnewPtr);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make sure `cold` owns the buffer holding the final state.\n    if (coldPtr != cold.data()) {\n        cold.swap(cnew);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n    // nullptr : cnewPtr);","newString":"    free(cnewPtr);","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <sched.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n// Read the sibling list of a hardware thread (e.g. \"0,128\" or \"0-1\") and return the\n// smallest CPU id in it, i.e. a stable representative of the physical core.\nint coreRepresentative(const int cpu) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/thread_siblings_list\", cpu);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) {\n        return cpu;\n    }\n    char buf[512] = {0};\n    const size_t len = fread(buf, 1, sizeof(buf) - 1, f);\n    fclose(f);\n    if (len == 0) {\n        return cpu;\n    }\n\n    int rep = cpu;\n    for (const char* p = buf; *p != '\\0';) {\n        if (*p >= '0' && *p <= '9') {\n            const int v = atoi(p);\n            rep = std::min(rep, v);\n            while (*p >= '0' && *p <= '9') ++p;\n        } else {\n            ++p;\n        }\n    }\n    return rep;\n}\n#endif\n\n// Pin every OpenMP thread to a distinct CPU of the process' affinity mask, and by default\n// use one thread per physical core.\n//\n// This benchmark is memory bound, so thread migration (which invalidates the NUMA-local\n// first touch of the grid arrays and the private caches) costs more than an order of\n// magnitude on large multi-socket machines, and SMT siblings only add contention. If the\n// user configured the OpenMP runtime's own binding or thread count we leave that alone;\n// otherwise we do it here, since libgomp has already parsed its environment by the time\n// main() runs.\nvoid configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Split the usable CPUs into one hardware thread per physical core plus the remaining\n    // SMT siblings.\n    std::vector<int> primary;\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if (coreRepresentative(cpu) == cpu) {\n            primary.push_back(cpu);\n        } else {\n            siblings.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cpuOrder = primary;\n    cpuOrder.insert(cpuOrder.end(), siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n    if (cpuOrder.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && getenv(\"OMP_THREAD_LIMIT\") == nullptr &&\n        !primary.empty()) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(primary.size()));\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        // Teams that fit on the physical cores are spread evenly over them (so that a\n        // partially loaded machine uses all caches, memory controllers and sockets);\n        // larger teams additionally fill the SMT siblings.\n        const int cpu = (nthreads <= primary.size() && !primary.empty())\n                            ? primary[tid * primary.size() / nthreads]\n                            : cpuOrder[tid % cpuOrder.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if dividing by v is exactly equivalent to multiplying by 1/v, which is the case\n// iff v is a (finite, normal) power of two.\ninline bool divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(const double v) noexcept {\n    int exp = 0;\n    return v > 0.0 && std::isnormal(v) && std::frexp(v, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Apply a stencil-based kernel to every cell of the grid, one (z, y) row at a time.\n//\n// The stencil uses clamped (zero-gradient) boundary conditions. Instead of clamping every\n// single access, the row pointers for the y/z neighbours are clamped once per row and only\n// the two x-boundary cells of a row need special treatment. This leaves a contiguous,\n// branch-free inner loop over x that the compiler can vectorize, and the (z, y) rows are\n// distributed over the OpenMP team with a static schedule so that every thread keeps\n// touching the same part of the grid across all time steps (NUMA locality).\n//\n// `body` is invoked as body(idx, laplacian) for every cell. With Recip the (loop\n// invariant) divisions by the squared grid spacings are turned into multiplications by\n// their reciprocals, which the caller only selects when that is bit-exact.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename F>\ninline void stencilLoop(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, F body) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const double dx2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    // Scale a second difference by the corresponding 1/h^2 factor.\n    const auto scale = [](const double v, const double h2) {\n        if constexpr (Recip) {\n            return v * h2;\n        } else {\n            return v / h2;\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Orphaned worksharing construct: this is called from inside a parallel region.\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ row = f + base;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowYp = f + z * nxy + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowYn = f + z * nxy + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : y) * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowZp = f + ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n            const double* __restrict__ rowZn = f + ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n\n            // Laplacian at position x of this row, given the clamped x neighbours.\n            const auto lap = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n                const double cv = row[x];\n                const double cxx = scale(row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n                const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n                const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n                return cxx + cyy + czz;\n            };\n\n            if (nx == 1) {\n                body(base, lap(0, 0, 0));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            body(base, lap(0, 1, 0));\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                const double cv = row[x];\n                const double cxx = scale(row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n                const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n                const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n                body(base + x, cxx + cyy + czz);\n            }\n\n            body(base + nx - 1, lap(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        const double cv = c[idx];\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                 + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                 - gamma * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field. The extra buffers are first-touched with the same\n// static (z, y) distribution used by the compute kernels so that all pages end up on the\n// NUMA node of the thread that will work on them.\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ cnew, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = base + x;\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = idx;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n                cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n                mu[idx] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict__ data = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i]);\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = data[0];\n    double maxVal = data[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    configureThreads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays. These are deliberately raw, uninitialized allocations: a\n    // std::vector would zero the whole grid from the master thread, which places every\n    // page on that thread's NUMA node. Instead the arrays are first touched below by the\n    // threads that will own them, which is worth over 50% of the achievable bandwidth on\n    // a multi-socket machine.\n    const size_t bytes = std::max<size_t>(gridSize * sizeof(double), 64);\n    double* coldPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    double* cnewPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    double* muPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    if (coldPtr == nullptr || cnewPtr == nullptr || muPtr == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grid arrays\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(coldPtr, cnewPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One single parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are created once and the\n    // per-step synchronization is just the implicit barriers of the worksharing constructs.\n    // Selected once: multiplying by the reciprocal grid spacings is only used when it\n    // yields exactly the same results as dividing.\n    const bool recip = divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dx * dx) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dy * dy) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dz * dz);\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            if (recip) {\n                computeChemicalPotential<true>(coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                               gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            } else {\n                computeChemicalPotential<false>(coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            }\n\n            // Update concentration\n            if (recip) {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<true>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            } else {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<false>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            std::swap(coldPtr, cnewPtr);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Hand the final state over for reporting. Orphaned worksharing construct: must be called from inside a parallel region.\ntemplate <typename RowFn>\ninline void forEachRow(const GridBlocking& b, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, RowFn rowFn) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zi = 0; zi < b.nzBlocks; ++zi) {\n        for (size_t yi = 0; yi < b.nyBlocks; ++yi) {\n            const size_t zEnd = std::min(nz, zi * b.zBlock + b.zBlock);\n            const size_t yEnd = std::min(ny, yi * b.yBlock + b.yBlock);\n            for (size_t z = zi * b.zBlock; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n                for (size_t y = yi * b.yBlock; y < yEnd; ++y) {\n                    rowFn(z, y);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply a stencil-based kernel to every cell of the grid, one (z, y) row at a time.\n//\n// The stencil uses clamped (zero-gradient) boundary conditions. Instead of clamping every\n// single access, the row pointers for the y/z neighbours are clamped once per row and only\n// the two x-boundary cells of a row need special treatment. This leaves a contiguous,\n// branch-free inner loop over x that the compiler can vectorize.\n//\n// `body` is invoked as body(idx, laplacian) for every cell. With Recip the (loop\n// invariant) divisions by the squared grid spacings are turned into multiplications by\n// their reciprocals, which the caller only selects when that is bit-exact.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename F>\ninline void stencilLoop(const double* __restrict__ f, const GridBlocking& b,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, F body) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const double dx2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    // Scale a second difference by the corresponding 1/h^2 factor.\n    const auto scale = [](const double v, const double h2) {\n        if constexpr (Recip) {\n            return v * h2;\n        } else {\n            return v / h2;\n        }\n    };\n\n    forEachRow(b, ny, nz, [&](const size_t z, const size_t y) {\n        const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ row = f + base;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowYp = f + z * nxy + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowYn = f + z * nxy + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : y) * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowZp = f + ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowZn = f + ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n\n        // Laplacian at position x of this row, given the clamped x neighbours.\n        const auto lap = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n            const double cv = row[x];\n            const double cxx = scale(row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n            const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n            const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n            return cxx + cyy + czz;\n        };\n\n        if (nx == 1) {\n            body(base, lap(0, 0, 0));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        body(base, lap(0, 1, 0));\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            const double cv = row[x];\n            const double cxx = scale(row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n            const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n            const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n            body(base + x, cxx + cyy + czz);\n        }\n\n        body(base + nx - 1, lap(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2));\n    });\n}\n\n'''\ns = s.replace(old, new)\n\n# kernels take the blocking\ns = s.replace(\"\"\"void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const size_t nx,\"\"\",\n\"\"\"void computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx,\"\"\")\ns = s.replace(\"stencilLoop<Recip>(c, nx\", \"stencilLoop<Recip>(c, b, nx\")\ns = s.replace(\"\"\"void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const size_t nx,\"\"\",\n\"\"\"void cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx,\"\"\")\ns = s.replace(\"stencilLoop<Recip>(mu, nx\", \"stencilLoop<Recip>(mu, b, nx\")\n\n# initialization uses the identical decomposition (NUMA first touch)\nold_init = s[s.index(\"// Initialize concentration field.\"):s.index(\"bool validateResult\")]\nnew_init = '''// Initialize concentration field. If the\n// user configured the OpenMP runtime's own binding or thread count we leave that alone;\n// otherwise we do it here, since libgomp has already parsed its environment by the time\n// main() runs.\nvoid configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Split the usable CPUs into one hardware thread per physical core plus the remaining\n    // SMT siblings.\n    std::vector<int> primary;\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if (coreRepresentative(cpu) == cpu) {\n            primary.push_back(cpu);\n        } else {\n            siblings.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cpuOrder = primary;\n    cpuOrder.insert(cpuOrder.end(), siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n    if (cpuOrder.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && getenv(\"OMP_THREAD_LIMIT\") == nullptr &&\n        !primary.empty()) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(primary.size()));\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        // Teams that fit on the physical cores are spread evenly over them (so that a\n        // partially loaded machine uses all caches, memory controllers and sockets);\n        // larger teams additionally fill the SMT siblings.\n        const int cpu = (nthreads <= primary.size() && !primary.empty())\n                            ? primary[tid * primary.size() / nthreads]\n                            : cpuOrder[tid % cpuOrder.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if dividing by v is exactly equivalent to multiplying by 1/v, which is the case\n// iff v is a (finite, normal) power of two.\ninline bool divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(const double v) noexcept {\n    int exp = 0;\n    return v > 0.0 && std::isnormal(v) && std::frexp(v, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the grid into (z-slab, y-block) tiles.\n//\n// Both the stencil sweeps and the initialization walk the grid with this decomposition and\n// a static schedule, so every thread always works on - and first touches - the same tiles.\nstruct GridBlocking {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyBlocks;\n    size_t nzBlocks;\n};\n\n// Pick a tile shape that keeps the three y/z neighbour row planes of a tile in cache while\n// still handing every thread a few tiles worth of work.\ninline GridBlocking makeBlocking(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int nthreads) {\n    // ~512 KiB of working set per thread: three planes of nx * yBlock doubles.\n    size_t yBlock = 512 * 1024 / (3 * nx * sizeof(double));\n    yBlock = std::clamp<size_t>(yBlock, 4, ny);\n\n    GridBlocking b{};\n    b.yBlock = yBlock;\n    b.nyBlocks = (ny + yBlock - 1) / yBlock;\n\n    // Over-decompose slightly in z so that the static schedule balances well.\n    const size_t wantedTiles = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(nthreads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzBlocks = std::clamp<size_t>((wantedTiles + b.nyBlocks - 1) / b.nyBlocks, 1, nz);\n    b.zBlock = (nz + nzBlocks - 1) / nzBlocks;\n    b.nzBlocks = (nz + b.zBlock - 1) / b.zBlock;\n    return b;\n}\n\n// Distribute the tiles over the OpenMP team and invoke rowFn(z, y) for every row of every\n// tile. Orphaned worksharing construct: must be called from inside a parallel region.\ntemplate <typename RowFn>\ninline void forEachRow(const GridBlocking& b, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, RowFn rowFn) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zi = 0; zi < b.nzBlocks; ++zi) {\n        for (size_t yi = 0; yi < b.nyBlocks; ++yi) {\n            const size_t zEnd = std::min(nz, zi * b.zBlock + b.zBlock);\n            const size_t yEnd = std::min(ny, yi * b.yBlock + b.yBlock);\n            for (size_t z = zi * b.zBlock; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n                for (size_t y = yi * b.yBlock; y < yEnd; ++y) {\n                    rowFn(z, y);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply a stencil-based kernel to every cell of the grid, one (z, y) row at a time.\n//\n// The stencil uses clamped (zero-gradient) boundary conditions. Instead of clamping every\n// single access, the row pointers for the y/z neighbours are clamped once per row and only\n// the two x-boundary cells of a row need special treatment. This leaves a contiguous,\n// branch-free inner loop over x that the compiler can vectorize.\n//\n// `body` is invoked as body(idx, laplacian) for every cell. With Recip the (loop\n// invariant) divisions by the squared grid spacings are turned into multiplications by\n// their reciprocals, which the caller only selects when that is bit-exact.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename F>\ninline void stencilLoop(const double* __restrict__ f, const GridBlocking& b,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, F body) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const double dx2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    // Scale a second difference by the corresponding 1/h^2 factor.\n    const auto scale = [](const double v, const double h2) {\n        if constexpr (Recip) {\n            return v * h2;\n        } else {\n            return v / h2;\n        }\n    };\n\n    forEachRow(b, ny, nz, [&](const size_t z, const size_t y) {\n        const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ row = f + base;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowYp = f + z * nxy + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowYn = f + z * nxy + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : y) * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowZp = f + ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowZn = f + ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n\n        // Laplacian at position x of this row, given the clamped x neighbours.\n        const auto lap = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n            const double cv = row[x];\n            const double cxx = scale(row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n            const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n            const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n            return cxx + cyy + czz;\n        };\n\n        if (nx == 1) {\n            body(base, lap(0, 0, 0));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        body(base, lap(0, 1, 0));\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            const double cv = row[x];\n            const double cxx = scale(row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n            const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n            const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n            body(base + x, cxx + cyy + czz);\n        }\n\n        body(base + nx - 1, lap(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2));\n    });\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(c, b, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        const double cv = c[idx];\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                 + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                 - gamma * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(mu, b, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field. The extra buffers are first-touched with the very same\n// tile distribution the compute kernels use, so all pages end up on the NUMA node of the\n// thread that will work on them.\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ cnew, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                             const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    forEachRow(b, ny, nz, [&](const size_t z, const size_t y) {\n        const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n            const size_t linear_id = idx;\n            const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n            c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n            mu[idx] = 0.0;\n        }\n    });\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict__ data = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i]);\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = data[0];\n    double maxVal = data[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    configureThreads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays. These are deliberately raw, uninitialized allocations: a\n    // std::vector would zero the whole grid from the master thread, which places every\n    // page on that thread's NUMA node. Instead the arrays are first touched below by the\n    // threads that will own them, which is worth over 50% of the achievable bandwidth on\n    // a multi-socket machine.\n    const size_t bytes = std::max<size_t>(gridSize * sizeof(double), 64);\n    double* coldPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    double* cnewPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    double* muPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    if (coldPtr == nullptr || cnewPtr == nullptr || muPtr == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grid arrays\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    const GridBlocking blocking = makeBlocking(nx, ny, nz, omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(coldPtr, cnewPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One single parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are created once and the\n    // per-step synchronization is just the implicit barriers of the worksharing constructs.\n    // Selected once: multiplying by the reciprocal grid spacings is only used when it\n    // yields exactly the same results as dividing.\n    const bool recip = divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dx * dx) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dy * dy) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dz * dz);\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            if (recip) {\n                computeChemicalPotential<true>(coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                               gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            } else {\n                computeChemicalPotential<false>(coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            }\n\n            // Update concentration\n            if (recip) {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<true>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            } else {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<false>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            std::swap(coldPtr, cnewPtr);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Hand the final state over for reporting. The scratch buffers are released first so\n    // that this does not increase the peak memory footprint.\n    free(muPtr);\n    free(cnewPtr);\n    std::vector<double> cold(coldPtr, coldPtr + gridSize);\n    free(coldPtr);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":137,"oldLines":13,"newStart":137,"newLines":15,"lines":[" // Pick a tile shape that keeps the three y/z neighbour row planes of a tile in cache while"," // still handing every thread a few tiles worth of work."," inline GridBlocking makeBlocking(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int nthreads) {","-    // ~512 KiB of working set per thread: three planes of nx * yBlock doubles.","-    size_t yBlock = 512 * 1024 / (3 * nx * sizeof(double));","-    yBlock = std::clamp<size_t>(yBlock, 4, ny);","+    // ~512 KiB of working set per thread: three planes of nx * yBlock doubles. If the\n// user configured the OpenMP runtime's own binding or thread count we leave that alone;\n// otherwise we do it here, since libgomp has already parsed its environment by the time\n// main() runs.\nvoid configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr ||\n        getenv(\"GOMP_CPU_AFFINITY\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    // Split the usable CPUs into one hardware thread per physical core plus the remaining\n    // SMT siblings.\n    std::vector<int> primary;\n    std::vector<int> siblings;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            continue;\n        }\n        if (coreRepresentative(cpu) == cpu) {\n            primary.push_back(cpu);\n        } else {\n            siblings.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cpuOrder = primary;\n    cpuOrder.insert(cpuOrder.end(), siblings.begin(), siblings.end());\n    if (cpuOrder.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && getenv(\"OMP_THREAD_LIMIT\") == nullptr &&\n        !primary.empty()) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(primary.size()));\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t nthreads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        // Teams that fit on the physical cores are spread evenly over them (so that a\n        // partially loaded machine uses all caches, memory controllers and sockets);\n        // larger teams additionally fill the SMT siblings.\n        const int cpu = (nthreads <= primary.size() && !primary.empty())\n                            ? primary[tid * primary.size() / nthreads]\n                            : cpuOrder[tid % cpuOrder.size()];\n        cpu_set_t set;\n        CPU_ZERO(&set);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &set);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(set), &set);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// True if dividing by v is exactly equivalent to multiplying by 1/v, which is the case\n// iff v is a (finite, normal) power of two.\ninline bool divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(const double v) noexcept {\n    int exp = 0;\n    return v > 0.0 && std::isnormal(v) && std::frexp(v, &exp) == 0.5;\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the grid into (z-slab, y-block) tiles.\n//\n// Both the stencil sweeps and the initialization walk the grid with this decomposition and\n// a static schedule, so every thread always works on - and first touches - the same tiles.\nstruct GridBlocking {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyBlocks;\n    size_t nzBlocks;\n};\n\n// Pick a tile shape that keeps the three y/z neighbour row planes of a tile in cache while\n// still handing every thread a few tiles worth of work.\ninline GridBlocking makeBlocking(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int nthreads) {\n    // ~512 KiB of working set per thread: three planes of nx * yBlock doubles. The block\n    // counts are turned back into evenly sized blocks so that all tiles carry the same\n    // amount of work.\n    const size_t yTarget = std::clamp<size_t>(512 * 1024 / (3 * nx * sizeof(double)), 4, ny);\n\n    GridBlocking b{};\n    b.nyBlocks = (ny + yTarget - 1) / yTarget;\n    b.yBlock = (ny + b.nyBlocks - 1) / b.nyBlocks;\n    b.nyBlocks = (ny + b.yBlock - 1) / b.yBlock;\n\n    // Over-decompose slightly in z so that the static schedule balances well.\n    const size_t wantedTiles = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(nthreads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzBlocks = std::clamp<size_t>((wantedTiles + b.nyBlocks - 1) / b.nyBlocks, 1, nz);\n    b.zBlock = (nz + nzBlocks - 1) / nzBlocks;\n    b.nzBlocks = (nz + b.zBlock - 1) / b.zBlock;\n    return b;\n}\n\n// Distribute the tiles over the OpenMP team and invoke rowFn(z, y) for every row of every\n// tile. Orphaned worksharing construct: must be called from inside a parallel region.\ntemplate <typename RowFn>\ninline void forEachRow(const GridBlocking& b, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, RowFn rowFn) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t zi = 0; zi < b.nzBlocks; ++zi) {\n        for (size_t yi = 0; yi < b.nyBlocks; ++yi) {\n            const size_t zEnd = std::min(nz, zi * b.zBlock + b.zBlock);\n            const size_t yEnd = std::min(ny, yi * b.yBlock + b.yBlock);\n            for (size_t z = zi * b.zBlock; z < zEnd; ++z) {\n                for (size_t y = yi * b.yBlock; y < yEnd; ++y) {\n                    rowFn(z, y);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Apply a stencil-based kernel to every cell of the grid, one (z, y) row at a time.\n//\n// The stencil uses clamped (zero-gradient) boundary conditions. Instead of clamping every\n// single access, the row pointers for the y/z neighbours are clamped once per row and only\n// the two x-boundary cells of a row need special treatment. This leaves a contiguous,\n// branch-free inner loop over x that the compiler can vectorize.\n//\n// `body` is invoked as body(idx, laplacian) for every cell. With Recip the (loop\n// invariant) divisions by the squared grid spacings are turned into multiplications by\n// their reciprocals, which the caller only selects when that is bit-exact.\ntemplate <bool Recip, typename F>\ninline void stencilLoop(const double* __restrict__ f, const GridBlocking& b,\n                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, F body) {\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n    const double dx2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dx * dx) : dx * dx;\n    const double dy2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dy * dy) : dy * dy;\n    const double dz2 = Recip ? 1.0 / (dz * dz) : dz * dz;\n\n    // Scale a second difference by the corresponding 1/h^2 factor.\n    const auto scale = [](const double v, const double h2) {\n        if constexpr (Recip) {\n            return v * h2;\n        } else {\n            return v / h2;\n        }\n    };\n\n    forEachRow(b, ny, nz, [&](const size_t z, const size_t y) {\n        const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ row = f + base;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowYp = f + z * nxy + ((y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y) * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowYn = f + z * nxy + ((y > 0) ? y - 1 : y) * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowZp = f + ((z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n        const double* __restrict__ rowZn = f + ((z > 0) ? z - 1 : z) * nxy + y * nx;\n\n        // Laplacian at position x of this row, given the clamped x neighbours.\n        const auto lap = [&](const size_t x, const size_t xp, const size_t xn) {\n            const double cv = row[x];\n            const double cxx = scale(row[xp] + row[xn] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n            const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n            const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n            return cxx + cyy + czz;\n        };\n\n        if (nx == 1) {\n            body(base, lap(0, 0, 0));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        body(base, lap(0, 1, 0));\n\n#pragma omp simd\n        for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n            const double cv = row[x];\n            const double cxx = scale(row[x + 1] + row[x - 1] - 2.0 * cv, dx2);\n            const double cyy = scale(rowYp[x] + rowYn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dy2);\n            const double czz = scale(rowZp[x] + rowZn[x] - 2.0 * cv, dz2);\n            body(base + x, cxx + cyy + czz);\n        }\n\n        body(base + nx - 1, lap(nx - 1, nx - 1, nx - 2));\n    });\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid computeChemicalPotential(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                              const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(c, b, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        const double cv = c[idx];\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                 + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                 - gamma * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\ntemplate <bool Recip>\nvoid cahnHilliardUpdate(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                        const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    stencilLoop<Recip>(mu, b, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, [=](const size_t idx, const double laplacian) {\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * laplacian;\n    });\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field. The extra buffers are first-touched with the very same\n// tile distribution the compute kernels use, so all pages end up on the NUMA node of the\n// thread that will work on them.\nvoid initializeConcentration(double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ cnew, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                             const GridBlocking& b, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n    const size_t nxy = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    forEachRow(b, ny, nz, [&](const size_t z, const size_t y) {\n        const size_t base = z * nxy + y * nx;\n        for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n            const size_t idx = base + x;\n            // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n            const size_t linear_id = idx;\n            const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n            c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            cnew[idx] = 0.0;\n            mu[idx] = 0.0;\n        }\n    });\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t n = c.size();\n    const double* __restrict__ data = c.data();\n\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bad = bad || std::isnan(data[i]) || std::isinf(data[i]);\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = data[0];\n    double maxVal = data[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, data[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, data[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Time steps: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    configureThreads();\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Physical parameters\n    const double dx = 1.0;\n    const double dy = 1.0;\n    const double dz = 1.0;\n    const double dt = 0.01;\n    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate arrays. These are deliberately raw, uninitialized allocations: a\n    // std::vector would zero the whole grid from the master thread, which places every\n    // page on that thread's NUMA node. Instead the arrays are first touched below by the\n    // threads that will own them, which is worth over 50% of the achievable bandwidth on\n    // a multi-socket machine.\n    const size_t bytes = std::max<size_t>(gridSize * sizeof(double), 64);\n    double* coldPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    double* cnewPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    double* muPtr = static_cast<double*>(aligned_alloc(64, (bytes + 63) & ~size_t(63)));\n    if (coldPtr == nullptr || cnewPtr == nullptr || muPtr == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate grid arrays\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    const GridBlocking blocking = makeBlocking(nx, ny, nz, omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Initialize concentration field\n    printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\n\");\n    initializeConcentration(coldPtr, cnewPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One single parallel region for the whole time loop: threads are created once and the\n    // per-step synchronization is just the implicit barriers of the worksharing constructs.\n    // Selected once: multiplying by the reciprocal grid spacings is only used when it\n    // yields exactly the same results as dividing.\n    const bool recip = divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dx * dx) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dy * dy) &&\n                       divisionIsExactlyReciprocal(dz * dz);\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {\n            // Compute chemical potential\n            if (recip) {\n                computeChemicalPotential<true>(coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                               gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            } else {\n                computeChemicalPotential<false>(coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz,\n                                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);\n            }\n\n            // Update concentration\n            if (recip) {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<true>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            } else {\n                cahnHilliardUpdate<false>(cnewPtr, coldPtr, muPtr, blocking, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);\n            }\n\n            // Swap buffers\n#pragma omp single\n            std::swap(coldPtr, cnewPtr);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance\n    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Hand the final state over for reporting. The scratch buffers are released first so\n    // that this does not increase the peak memory footprint.\n    free(muPtr);\n    free(cnewPtr);\n    std::vector<double> cold(coldPtr, coldPtr + gridSize);\n    free(coldPtr);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(cold, \"Concentration\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":137,"oldLines":7,"newStart":137,"newLines":7,"lines":[" // Pick a tile shape that keeps the three y/z neighbour row planes of a tile in cache while"," // still handing every thread a few tiles worth of work."," inline GridBlocking makeBlocking(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int nthreads) {","-    // ~512 KiB of working set per thread: three planes of nx * yBlock doubles.
